import { wrapClient, optionIndex, withOption } from "base-ajax/plugins";

export function instrument(client, options = {}) {
  const stats = { count: 0, totalMs: 0, errors: 0 };

  const middleware = (originalFn, method) => (...args) => {
    // 1) Ù…ÛŒâ€ŒØªÙˆØ§Ù†ÛŒØ¯ Ø¢Ø±Ú¯ÙˆÙ…Ø§Ù†â€ŒÙ‡Ø§ Ø±Ø§ ØªØºÛŒÛŒØ± Ø¯Ù‡ÛŒØ¯ (index Ø´ÛŒØ¡ options)
    if (method !== "REQUEST") {
      const index = optionIndex(method);
      const current = args[index] || {};
      const headers = { ...(current.headers || {}), "X-Client": "web" };
      args = withOption(args, index, { ...current, headers });
    }

    // 2) Ù‚Ø¨Ù„ Ùˆ Ø¨Ø¹Ø¯ Ø§Ø² ÙØ±Ø§Ø®ÙˆØ§Ù†ÛŒ ÙˆØ§Ù‚Ø¹ÛŒ
    const startedAt = performance.now();
    stats.count += 1;
    return originalFn(...args).then(
      (result) => {
        stats.totalMs += performance.now() - startedAt;
        options.onSuccess?.(method);
        return result;
      },
      (error) => {
        stats.errors += 1;
        options.onError?.(error, method);
        throw error;
      },
    );
  };

  // 3) handle Ø±Ø§ Ø¨Ø§ accessor Ø¨Ø¯Ù‡ÛŒØ¯ ØªØ§ Ø²Ù†Ø¯Ù‡ Ø¨Ù…Ø§Ù†Ø¯
  return wrapClient(client, middleware, {
    get stats() {
      return { ...stats, avgMs: stats.count ? stats.totalMs / stats.count : 0 };
    },
  });
}

// Ø§Ø³ØªÙØ§Ø¯Ù‡

await api.get("/users");
console.log(api.stats);   // { count: 1, totalMs: 12, errors: 0, avgMs: 12 }

import BaseAjax, { cache, compose, circuitBreaker, dedupe, queue, trace } from "base-ajax/all";

const base = BaseAjax.createClient({
  baseURL: "/api",
  timeout: 10_000,
  retry: { count: 2, delay: 300, jitter: true },
  auth: { mode: "memory", refreshUrl: "/auth/refresh" },
  correlationHeader: "X-Request-Id",
  logLevel: "warn",
});

  base,
  (c) => queue(c, { maxConcurrent: 6 }),
  (c) => dedupe(c),
  (c) => cache(c, { ttl: 30_000, staleWhileRevalidate: true }),
  (c) => circuitBreaker(c, { failureThreshold: 5, cooldownMs: 20_000 }),
  (c) => trace(c, { onTrace: (v) => (window.__lastTrace = v) }),
);

const base = BaseAjax.createClient({
  baseURL: import.meta.env.VITE_API_URL || "/api",
  timeout: 12_000,
  responseType: "json",
  credentials: "include",                  // Ú©ÙˆÚ©ÛŒ HttpOnly
  csrf: { cookieName: "XSRF-TOKEN", headerName: "X-XSRF-TOKEN" },
  retry: { count: 2, delay: 300, jitter: true, statuses: [429, 502, 503, 504] },
  auth: {
    mode: "cookie",
    onUnauthorized: () => router.push("/login"),
  },
  correlationHeader: "X-Request-Id",
  allowedOrigins: ["https://auth.example.com"],
  logLevel: import.meta.env.DEV ? "debug" : "error",
});

  (c) => queue(c, { maxConcurrent: 6 }),
  (c) => dedupe(c),
  (c) => cache(c, { ttl: 30_000, staleWhileRevalidate: true }),
  (c) => circuitBreaker(c, { failureThreshold: 5, cooldownMs: 20_000 }),
  (c) => trace(c),
);

export async function download(path, filename) {
  const blob = await api.get(path, undefined, { responseType: "blob", bustCache: true });
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const link = document.createElement("a");
  link.href = url;
  link.download = filename || path.split("/").pop();
  link.click();
  URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
}
